# 1997 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** PointCast push · ICQ UIN · eBay bid
**Star:** PointCast · `itt97-pointcast` · `sites/pointcast/index.html`
**ILS June:** 1,117,255 · 120,758,310
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 364
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt97-pointcast`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/1997/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/1997/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 1997 — browser war · push
2. PointCast — subscribe News + Weather REAL
3. ICQ — sign on (UIN theater)
4. eBay laptop — bid higher
5. HoTMaiL · Slashdot
6. Year flow map

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

---

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt97-pointcast`

**Room:** `years/1997/sites/pointcast/index.html`
**Hook:** `data-pc-sub`
**Nostalgia:** PointCast — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- 0 channels · App Store-as-1997 trap never write.
- Leftover panel on this room (if any) writes a leftover key, **not** `itt97-pointcast`.

**Complete writes**
- Subscribe News + Weather · honesty. Writes itt97-pointcast.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `1997` · key `itt97-pointcast`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-pointcast` — do not write `itt96-*` or `itt98-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** ICQ sign-on → `years/1997/sites/icq/index.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

## L · Leftover-official — every dest · 364 flows

Protocol is the same machine (`js/immersion/leftover-official.js`). The minutes below are dest-true: key, needPick, field, next, hops.

### L-0001 · `itt97-aim-birth` · sites/aim/index.html

**Open:** `/years/1997/sites/aim/index.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="aim-birth"])`
**Need:** field ≥2 (placeholder “aim-birth leftover”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `yahoo` · `amazon` · `ebay`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="aim-birth"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt97-aim-birth']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”
7. `itt97-pointcast` stays empty. `itt96-aim-birth` and `itt98-aim-birth` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “aim-birth leftover”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="aim-birth"]`.
- `localStorage['itt97-aim-birth']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"1997", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt97-aim-birth`.
- Does **not** write `itt97-pointcast`.
- **Next:** Starting Point → `/years/1997/pages/home.html`

- Status: `Saved · itt97-ms-lx`.

**Incomplete never writes**

3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Click `[data-lo-pick="keep"]`. Wrong pick never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="mp3-d2"]`.
- `localStorage['itt97-mp3-d2']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"1997", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt97-mp3-d2`.
- Does **not** write `itt97-pointcast`.
